## v0.0.18 (2026-11-16)

- **Fix names the shortest honest way to fire from what you believe of the gun, for any gun** (your ask of 2026-11-15: "I feel like the reload, top up etc options should simply look at the state of our weapons"). A planner (`src/sim/arms/plan.js`) searches the believed gun (the magazine in or out and its count as 0, some or unknown, the chamber, the bolt, any stoppage, the selector, a magazine in the hand, the best in the pouches and at the feet) with `firearms.js`'s own actions on a witness built from the belief, so it knows only what you know. Every handling row takes its path from it: RELOAD, Top up, RELOAD keep the empty, IMMEDIATE and REMEDIAL ACTION and the core's Fix. The ids, the tower's commands (LOAD, MAKE READY, UNLOAD, SHOW CLEAR, CEASE FIRE, MAKE SAFE) and every R-7 path the bots, the instinct and the gauges key on are as built. A walk over every belief the hands reach (1,485 beliefs, R-7 936 and P-12 549, times 8 pouch states) finds 0 dead ends: a way in 11,110 of 11,880, honestly none in 770. v0.0.17's Fix had no way in 224 of the beliefs it was given. The mean way is 2.67 s at Weapon handling 10 (worst 6.11 s); a plan takes 0.18 to 0.30 ms cold on average over the round's runs and is then memoised, and the panel at the house's contact stop is planned in about 1.2 ms cold, against `PLAN_CEIL_MS` 3 ms times k.
  - **A magazine believed empty in a closed, empty rifle is no longer a dead end.** v0.0.17 offered nothing (MAKE READY wanted rounds believed in, RELOAD a locked bolt, Top up rounds) and Fix read "nothing offered now". Now RELOAD is Fix: drop it, reach, seat, charge, 3.11 s at Weapon handling 10. An empty magazine in the hand is dropped, never seated; with "RELOAD, keep the empty" it is dumped to the pouch.
  - **A fumbled lock back no longer leads to a double feed.** The round left behind the locked bolt reads "chamber unknown", and Fix is "Look at the port" (0.3 s), then Charge. Before it was Top up, and the bolt release then fed a second round. In the shoot house an empty chamber with a magazine in is Charge (0.92 s), not MAKE READY, which went to safe first; on the instructor's MAKE READY Fix is still MAKE READY, and on the bays and the tower's lines Fix is v0.0.17's except for the look on a chamber unknown with no command current. Whether to look first or Charge at once is question 1 of DESIGN.md's log of 2026-11-16; look first stands until you answer.
  - **Carry on picks up from where the gun is now.** A RELOAD whose seat was fumbled and the magazine dropped offered "Carry on: Bolt release" on no magazine; now "Carry on: Reach for a magazine, Magazine in, Bolt release", 1.96 s, and again after a second fumble inside that Carry on (the boot recruit on seed 16). A reach that feels the magazine empty drops it and reaches again.
  - **Over a ready gun in a fight Fix is "Nothing to fix".** It read Top up, a 2.1 s magazine change, at 19 of 21 of the boot recruit's contact pauses; Top up stays on GUN 1.
- **The P-12 is shown and handled like the rifle** (your ask of 2026-11-15: "if we have sidearms, they should somehow show up like the main weapon, showing if holstered or no but should have a chamber and all the sequencing the main weapon has"). The weapon card shows both guns: the gun in use whole with a brass hands mark, the other in a strip at the card's foot (rows 123 to 147, the rule at 123) with where it is (holster, hanging on its sling, slung), its silhouette and its condition from what you believe of it ("SAFE 1+9", an amber "!" for a stoppage, "?" for a chamber never checked), and a click on the strip is Transition or Rifle up. With one gun the card is v0.0.17's to the pixel (21 cases pinned). Three ways to show it are in `samples/wcard2` (WC2-A is built); the P-12 drawn is a pistol (P12A, the service pistol, until you score `samples/p12-card`), never the R-7 carrying the pistol's belief as before.
  - **The P-12 has its own words and rows.** Rack the slide, Slide release, Lock the slide back, "Reach: the pistol pouch" (a row read "reach@pp"); RELOAD and Top up from the thigh pouch, its empty magazine back in the thigh pouch and not a rifle's chest pouch; no "Sling it" and no "Port cover" on it. Drawn, its RELOAD takes 2.84, 2.20, 1.85, 1.65, 1.23 and 1.00 s from the boot recruit to the tier one. Gun down and Off the line act on the gun in use, so a stopped P-12 is tapped and racked, never the hanging rifle.
  - **Rifle up brings the rifle up ready.** Its tail is the rifle's own way to ready ("Rifle up: holster, RELOAD" for a rifle gone dry, "Magazine in" with its magazine out, tap and rack for a stoppage), to the ms on every sheet (4,902, 3,868, 3,305, 2,968, 2,296 and 1,923 ms on a click, v0.0.17's).
  - **The holstered P-12 has rows of its own in GUN ▸ "Hands ▸"**: P-12: Press check, Magazine check, Top up and MAKE READY (drawn, done and holstered on safe, the rifle up again; the sergeant's press check 3.67 s), on the lane with nobody facing you, never on the band.
  - **A fumbled holster leaves the P-12 in your hand and says so.** "P-12 misses the holster: still in your hand." (it said "slips out of your hand and falls" and holstered it on safe); a fumbled draw "snags in the holster: it stays in."; Rifle up is offered again.
  - **The tower waits on an unchambered P-12 before GO.** Under MAKE READY or Standby with the rifle ready and the holstered P-12's round not up, the instructor says "Shooter, your pistol too: a round up.", the panel offers "P-12: MAKE READY" and the DI names it (the boot recruit on 9052 reached GO with it empty, without a word). The tower's move-on still gives GO.
- **Fix, T and the DI agree when the gun cannot fire** (the play review and the dead-end hunt). With the rifle on its sling in a fight, Fix is Rifle up (it was "Nothing to fix" and T did nothing, Up four keys deep), the DI says "Rifle's on the sling. Up."; hanging on a stoppage with the P-12 holstered, Transition (the draw alone) is offered, and Gun down and Off the line, whose drills the slung rifle refuses, are not. On a bolt locked back over a magazine with rounds the DI says "Bolt release." and T releases it (it looped on Top up for ever); on Condition 3 "Charge it."; on the P-12 drawn unchambered "Rack it."; on a chamber unknown behind a locked bolt "Check the port."; the shot lines keep quiet until the gun can fire. With every R-7 magazine believed empty and a loaded P-12 holstered Transition is offered and the DI says "Dry. Transition.", not "Reload." over nothing to reload. A P-12 magazine held on the R-7 is never seated: "Magazine in" is offered only for the gun's own families, and a seat of another is refused with "That magazine doesn't fit the R-7." instead of breaking the tick.
  - **Off the lane Fix follows the instructor.** Before GO and after the lane Fix is LOAD, MAKE READY, CEASE FIRE or UNLOAD, SHOW CLEAR where the tower has said it, else "Nothing to fix · the line is cold": never Off safe, a Charge before the tower's MAKE READY, or a Top up. Off the shoot house with no command current, the in-0 state on safe is RELOAD where no round is believed chambered (it was Off safe first, a press with nothing to fire).

- **The I and C figures wear what you wear now** (your ask of 2026-11-15: "the I and C menu preview of the character should show the currently worn gear"): in the shoot house the combat EVA suit, the plate carrier with a baseplate for each magazine in its pouches, the dump pouch on the belt, the P-12 in its thigh holster with its spare below, and the R-7 at the ready. One record (`src/sim/arms/gear.js` `gearOf`) reads the kit and is drawn by the doll (`dress.js`, `worn.js`): the rifle slung front, on the back or in both hands at the low ready (sample R), the P-12 at the ready after Transition with the holster's mouth open, ear defenders' cups where they are worn, a magazine in the hand. It holds nothing the eye cannot see (no count, load, chamber, selector or stoppage). The inventory's plaque says COMBAT EVA SUIT. A dressed doll paints in about 1.5 ms at the hit card's near band (1.0 ms at the I card's scale; the budget 4 ms times k), the rifle's pixels memoised once (it was 5 to 7 ms). A loadout's devices are drawn where they are worn: Flash's two G-11s on the carrier's left flank, Breach's strip charges and firing devices on the right, the halligan's bar and fork over the right shoulder (the viewer's left), the ram's head over the left.
  - **The combat EVA suit is drawn on the doll at last.** v0.0.17's doll drew the utilities under it (8,960 of 8,960 pixels the same), and the indoor rule would have taken its sealed helmet off under the Wheel's roof; the suit is black, its visor down, indoors too. - **The target view opens whenever you engage a stand or a man in the shoot house, and a man anywhere but "Bang"** (your words of 2026-11-15: "we need to generalize that target view for every time we're engaging something or someone"). It opens on the row or Fire under way, the aim, or the bracket at a pause once your take-in has seen the stand, and shows the stand as your take-in identified it, never as its record holds ("HANDS NOT SEEN" when your look failed), your rounds as called and seen during the lane; held 6 s out of stop motion, closed when the stand turns away after "Clear!", hidden from Stop. A stand facing a G-11 within 60 degrees is blinded for 5 s, otherwise startled for 1.25 to 2.55 s (a cell member's hardening, H 8); its turn waits for its daze and its par runs from its end ("1A BLINDED 5.00 S"). In the combat EVA suit and on your own call, you and your #2 feel under 10 and are never startled or blinded.

Then prime, "Breaching!", the 2.5 s count and the fire. The stack takes 27.1 kPa (you) and 16.9 kPa (your #2), under the limit, and the paper says DOOR BLOWN. A misfire (the FD-1's 1 in 100) hands back "Misfire. Prime it again."; a charge left on the door is CHARGE LEFT ON THE DOOR, a fault at the lane's end ("Never walk away from a placed charge."). On v0.0.17 its 432 lanes met 3,618 cases, every one a known dead end of the hunt's; this release fixes 17 of those 19 classes, whose 38 saved dead-end states (`tests/fumble-fixtures.json.br`) now pin the fixes. Still met on v0.0.18's 216 lanes, accepted and printed as open (`OPEN`) for the next rounds: the body-gap pin (32 cases in 16 lanes, the random bot's only), the P-12 not in Condition 1 at GO after a fumbled MAKE READY (7), T leading on over a dry gun (4), T going between Rifle up and Transition with both guns dry (1), and one misreading of the net's own (3); and still known, the DI's no-shoot line with no act over an empty gun (16 cases).

## v0.0.17 (2026-11-15)

- **Every sheet carries the P-12: one loadout, the boot recruit's as the tier one's.** Your words of 2026-11-15: "everyone should have the same loadout, there is no reason to not give sidearms to low ranks". The boot recruit and the private now draw the thigh holster, a HAD-17 of ten frangible and a spare, which before went only to the sergeant and up; every sheet's fill has the same 22 ids and weighs 26.89 kg. Transition: pistol is on every jam card, at 1.40 s times the sheet's pace: the boot recruit 1.81 s, the private 1.40, the corporal 1.18, the sergeant 1.05, the Raider 0.78, the tier one 0.64. "Not issued" is gone; only a man with no pistol at all sees "not carried". Older house saves gain the P-12 on load, in the condition the tower's commands so far would leave it: Condition 3 before MAKE READY, Condition 1 on safe until UNLOAD, Condition 4 after. Your number two still carries the rifle alone (my call, for you to overrule: his P-12 would need his own admin at each tower command, and with you covering him his answer at a jam is Gun down).
- **Below rung 2 the DI's answer at the click is Tap, rack.** That is what training teaches first. Before, it was the card's first answer: "He's close. Pistol." led the boot recruit into P-12 head shots at Marksmanship 10, and on seed 1037 twenty missed the plate in a row. The card's order is unchanged, and Transition is one press on it. While an answer runs, the line says that answer: "He's close. Pistol." during a transition, never "It quit. Tap, rack.".
- **A dry gun's line is RELOAD, named with its row, ahead of any shot line.** With the bolt locked back, or the chamber empty and no magazine in, "Plate. Go to the head.", "Hostage. Head only, or hold." and "Press." stand down, and "Empty. Reload." names its row: Transition where the P-12 is holstered and a man is close, RELOAD, Magazine in, the bolt release, or "Pistol's dry. Rifle up." where nothing is left to load. Before, those lines named no row and T did nothing, and the boot recruit walked room 3 and the hall on an empty pistol. With the chamber unknown after Rifle up, the line is the press check. At the tower's Stop, CEASE FIRE and UNLOAD with the rifle hanging on its sling, the line now names Rifle up; before, "The tower said UNLOAD, SHOW CLEAR." named nothing.
- **A threat's pistol is "His pistol." in the DI's words** ("His pistol. On him."), never the bare "Pistol." the transition says.
- **Your number two comes into room 2 on his cue (your report: "squad AI sometimes stays out of the room on entry (room 2)").** His doorway check now ends 0.5 m past the doorway, not at his point 1.5 m in. When you let go just inside, your feet are carried off his way in. If his take-in cuts his entry short, he goes on in instead of back to his stack. The worst case by T was 4.80 s from his cue to the threshold; it is now 2.95 s. If you stand on his way in after his cue, he calls **"Coming in!"** after 1 s, once a hold. Before, he waited without a word: in one fuzz room, 11.9 s.
- **A door's swing now decides the entry.** Each door is read from its own data: push or pull from the stack's side, and its hinge side. The stack forms on the latch side, and #1 opens the door. From rung 3 he shoves a push door on the approach's last 0.6 m and follows it in at two thirds open. At a pull door he stops, pulls (0.3 s more, times your pace) and waits until it is fully open. Twenty-seed lane means, before and after: the sergeant 48.6 to 49.2 s, the Raider 42.9 to 43.1 s, the tier one 41.7 to 42.0 s. The corporal and every lane by the keys are unchanged.
  - **Kick door only on a push door** (your ask, 2026-11-15). On room 2's pull door, Kick is struck "opens toward you" and Push/Kick is not offered. A pull door opens from its latch side: from the hinge side, Open is struck "from the latch side". Open reads "Open the door: pull".
  - **Read the door says how it opens**: "Pull door, hinge right." at room 2; "Push door, hinge left." at rooms 1 and 3, and at the closet seen from room 2.
  - **The swing is drawn**: a shut door's arc as a dotted rust quarter circle at wall-cut height, and while a leaf moves, the rest of its sweep in red, a place not to stop.
- **Your corner has a rule, and the floor shows it** (your words of 2026-11-15: "there should be rigorous definition of which is our corner, because there is currently no indication at to where we're supposed to be looking when entering a room"). Each room's door is read as centre-fed, corner-fed or a passage (src/sim/combat/corner.js). #1 goes to the open side at a corner-fed door, to the deeper side where the room is uneven, behind the door at a push door, and to the latch side at a pull door. #2 goes the other way. His corner is the first danger area: the near corner he walks into, or the far corner of his wall when he goes straight in. His sector runs from the far corner on his side to the far wall's middle. In house 1, room 1 is left (cross), room 2 left (hook) with the #2 straight in, room 3 right (the room's L shape), and in the hall each man goes down his own wall watching the far corner across.
  - **From the read of the door: a teal bracket in your corner and a ring at your point, with your #2's dotted and grey; from your entry, a thin wedge of your sector to the walls.** They are drawn flat on the room's floor in an overlay pass, over the walls that would hide them, and the fog never dims them. The first draft sat a quarter metre over the cut walls, so room 2's marks read as being in the hall. They fade when the room is dominated. The boot recruit keeps them until domination; the hold is 20 s for the private, 10 s for the corporal, 5 s for the sergeant and 2.5 s for the Raider and the tier one, and the fade takes 0.8 s.
  - **The DI names your corner once, at the stack, as the screen shows it**: "Your corner: left." at room 1 and "right" at rooms 2 and 3. Room 2 is entered going south, so the marine's own left is the screen's right, where its bracket is drawn. At the open door the side is not repeated.
- **The step panel is drawn as your pick, P3 (layout C)** (your words of 2026-11-15: "the keys selection isn't updated according to the choices made earlier yet"). It has two rows of six tiles: the situation's tile with its picture and name, then "Tab held: more", and the core's FIX and MUZZLE named. Holding Tab shows a half-wheel of the five groups beside the group's card and its nine. At 1024 px each tile says one word. The keys card describes only the layout played, and the menu toggle goes from C to A.
  - At the door the five are as P3 drew them: Stack up 1, Stack up and take point 2, Open 3, Kick door 4, Enter 5 (before, Enter was 4 and Kick 5).
  - What is done stays on its key, ticked as already done: stacked, the door read, the hall's end reached, his hands seen, a door already open. An inner door keeps the room's keys on 1 to 3, with Hold it on 4 and Clear it on 5. The hall is named THE HALL and IN THE HALL.
  - **The DI's line shows the keys of the row it names after its words, and that tile is edged in brass.** Press names the shot or Fire. Where the act is that same row, T's label is no longer said before the words. Before, at 1600 × 900 "T: Open the door: pull · 1.0 s." cut "Your corner" and the key chip with "…".
  - In Pairs and Three bangs, when the target is still up the line reads "To the head: 4", tile 4 is edged in brass, and Pair, Failure and Fire are dimmed (your question of 2026-11-14).
- **In Pairs and Three bangs, after a man falls the line names the next man's aim and T aims at him.** Before, "He is down. Next: the rifle, lane one." had no act and no row. T showed nothing, the aim stayed on the man who fell, and only Q or D then Enter went on, which nothing named. This happened three times a course on every sheet. Now the panel's aim row goes to the next man up that you can see: the line reads "He is down. Next: the rifle, lane one. Aim: 7.", and T aims.
- **After "Move on." a leg is passed 30 s later unless it moves on (my call).** The 30 s run from the words, or from the leg's own last step after them (stacked, opened, entered). A look at a corner or a round fired no longer buys more time; before, each one did, and the fuzz's lanes ran to the 600 s limit.
- **A rifle with its magazine out always has a way to load it in a fight.** Your report from the shoot house: "fumbled magazine when topping up, no option to load", then "fired the last bullet and still no option to reload". A top-up whose old magazine slipped and fell left the rifle with no magazine and a round chambered. The panel then offered only CEASE FIRE and UNLOAD, and Fix read "Nothing to fix". Once that round was fired, only the tower's LOAD was offered: on safe, to the port, no charge, and not on the contact panel (Fix showed a struck eye). Now a magazine out with a round chambered offers **Magazine in**: the reach and the seat, 1.6 s for the private. A magazine out on a dry chamber offers **RELOAD**: the reach, the seat and Charge, or the bolt release when the bolt is locked, 2.5 s for the private. Either one leads the reload slot and Fix, and a man facing you no longer pushes it back.
- **The test runner no longer slows down just because your game's server is up.** Your words: "I've not been playing for hours". The pool was held to 3 workers whenever the play copy answered on :8200, all day. It is now held only while the Mac has also had keyboard or mouse input in the last 5 minutes (`tests/run.mjs` `PLAYING_IDLE_S`, read from macOS's HIDIdleTime); otherwise 7 workers.
- **The gunshot is rebuilt to bang harder** (your words of 2026-11-15: "guns should bang harder and closer to realistic"). It is made of a blast that peaks in 0.04 ms, the bullet's crack, the action's clacks, and the space's answer. In the shoot house that is dense hard-wall reflections (RT60 0.55 s); on the 25 m and 300 m ranges a dry rolling echo and the berm's slap-back. Through the defenders at 25 m it now peaks at -1.5 dBFS (before, -11.7). Score it by ear: `&sound=old` plays v0.0.15's sound to compare.
  - The R-7 and the P-12 now sound different. The rifle is heavier, with 64% of its energy under 200 Hz against 27%, and its carrier knocks at 34 and 74 ms. The pistol is brighter and shorter, its slide at 14 and 38 ms, and 2.9 dB quieter.
  - The suit's active hearing and the ear defenders now work like electronic hearing protection: the bang is clipped and compressed but stays hard and close. Other shooters are heard a sound's way late, by distance and side, with the fall in level halved while protection is worn.
  - A new master limiter keeps every sound under full scale: twelve open shots in 1.2 s peak at -1.4 dBFS. Its soft clip runs without oversampling, so its offline model is the whole path.
- **Steps are quieter** (your words of 2026-11-15: "steps are too loud"): 17 dB lower than before, 5 dB less again in Careful and 1 dB more in Fast. In the house a Fast step is about 30 dB under your shot (before, about 5).
- **The helmet candidates are in the gallery to score (samples/helmets, 21 views); the game's look is unchanged until you pick** (your words of 2026-11-15: "The helmet design is pretty goofy"). There are four regulars, four elites with the Mars and UN trims, and three OPA, beside today's helmet. Each candidate is shown on the stack walking in the shoot house's light, at the door from four sides at twice size, and on the card.
  - The regulars are a 17 px closed shell (today's dome is 19 px, 4 over the skull) with a wraparound visor lit faintly amber and a moulded jaw.
  - The elites are a 15 px faceless shell with lit slits or dots, red unless a faction sets the colour. The trim is Mars red #C8322A or UN blue #3A7AD8, on the helmet, the plate edges and the shoulder seams.
  - The OPA wear a cut helmet with rails over a full-face mask with two round side filters, in their own worn colours and with no faction trim.
- Tests: new `pairs-next` (the Next line's aim row on four courses), `house-transition` (heavy: the boot recruit and the private answering Transition, four seeds each, every lane to its card and no line naming no row), `house-words`, `house-block`, `house-entry` (3), `house-stall`, `house-doors` (10), `house-corner` (6), `house-magout` (2), `panel-keys` (11), `ui-panel-c` (2, heavy), `audio-gun` (9), `art-helmets` (5); flawless gains F25 (the #2 in on his cue, its margin to 3 s printed, enforced) and F26 (nobody in a door's swing while it moves: 0.0 s over 200 lanes, enforced); `house-sidearm` on every sheet; `game-upgrade` loads a new v0.0.15 save; `pairs-head` gains fuzz seed 3109, so the RELOAD line still fires now that RELOAD leads with the magazine out (it fired 0 times on seeds 3101 and 3104); `house-corner`'s words and pixels re-pinned on purpose (the screen's side, the floor overlay); `audio-browser` split into the new sound and `&sound=old`; `ui-panel` and `core-browser` updated. The keys bot presses Rifle up when the DI says "Rifle up.".

## v0.0.16 (2026-11-15)

- **Free arms swing as arms do, opposite their own legs and within 20 degrees walking and 34 running: no more flopping** (your report of 2026-11-15: "urgent : the walking animation ??? What is going on with the arms flopping around like that", "running also... wth"). An arm not holding the rifle (Relaxed, the rifle slung, or none) was driven by its thigh's angle, which carries the knee's lift: both arms swung back at once, up to 1.41 rad (81 degrees) in the walk and 2.30 rad (132 degrees) in the run. Now each arm swings by where its leg's foot is in the stride (src/art/figures/walk.js swingArms, ARM_SWING_RAD walk 0.35, run 0.6), the run's elbows bent near 80 degrees. A held rifle keeps both hands on it, as before.
- **Running on Mars and Europa flies a little longer, not in long slow leaps** (your "the running being long slow leaps is intentional ? Is it to show running in Mars gravity ?"). It was the gravity, overdone: the take-off speed was fixed at 0.9 m/s, so Mars's run flew 0.49 s and rose 10.9 cm, Europa's 1.37 s and 30.8 cm. Runners in low gravity push off less hard, so the take-off now scales with the square root of g (src/core/phys.js takeoffVzMs, my call): the apex is 4.1 cm in every gravity and the flight 0.18 s at 1 G, 0.30 s on Mars, 0.50 s on Europa; Mars's run 1.74 to 1.24 m a step, Europa's 3.14 to 1.43 m; 1 G (the shoot house) unchanged.
- Tests: walk-arms (new: the free arms in every cycle and band, held arms, the apex and flight by gravity); house-bodies' step table and Mars cadence re-pinned on purpose (Mars run under 0.75 of 1 G's, was 0.6).

## v0.0.15 (2026-11-14)

- **Two rounds of look samples wait in the gallery to be scored (`./play.sh --gallery`); the game's own look is unchanged until you pick.** None of it reaches the game: the figure's marks are drawn only for a look that carries `mark`, which no look of the game does, and a pistol design only for a card that names one (`PISTOL_DRAWN_AS` is empty, so a P-12 in hand still draws the R-7's card).
  - S, the stack's figures (samples/house-stack-figures): you and your #2 in the black combat EVA suit walking up to room 1's door, one behind the other, then your #2 crossing in front of you, over the game's own picture of the hall at 10:00 on seed 48213, an 8.7 s loop that moves when opened with O. S-a is today's look; S-b a grey edge on each leg, boot and the far arm; S-c a visor per man (yours smoked blue, your #2's gold); S-d the Navy's red tape on your #2 (helmet band, shoulders, near arm); S-e a pixel of the floor's tone where one man overlaps the other; S-f a hard leg shadow per man in place of the blob; S-g the six on one clock; S-h the check, today's look at GO beside the game's shot. Several can be joined.
  - P12, the P-12's drawing (src/art/pistols.js, samples/p12-card and samples/p12-draw): four designs on the weapon card at 432 px/m, three times the rifle's 144 (A the service pistol, B the slab, C the monolith, D the lanyard), each in eight beliefs (P12A to P12D: on safe with ten, about six left, locked back on empty, a stovepipe, never checked, a double feed, Condition 3, the magazine out); P12X-a the four side by side, P12X-b A in the seven finishes; P12M-a to P12M-f the pistol at 16 px/m in the figure's hand, drawn, fired twice and holstered on a 5.2 s loop (1.1 s draw, 1.3 s holster), in the suit and, for P12M-f, in utilities, where a black pistol is not lost on black.
  - On the check, two of the card's marks moved: the "?" of a magazine count never checked sat on the grip's front strap (now 4 px further forward, clear of it and its shadow), and "A FEW" in the hand ran to 1 px from the card's right edge (the hand's word now ends 4 px inside it, as MAG OUT does). The rifle's card is unchanged.
- Tests: no new test; the quick suite is green, 1,071 passed in 32.8 s. Every figure frame (63,375: ten outfits, with and without rifle, pistol and carrier, four facings, every frame at 16 and 24 px/m) and the R-7's and P-12's cards hash the same as v0.0.14's, the shoot house at GO, at contact and at room 2 is pixel for pixel v0.0.14's, and the shoot house bench runs without an error. The 24 views shoot whole pixels, and the eight moving views opened live change frame over 2.8 s with no page or console errors.

## v0.0.14 (2026-11-14)

- **The shoot house is rebuilt from your note on v0.0.12's "Stack and clear", the pilot for the whole game** (your scores of 2026-11-11, 25, 25 and 25: "This is the first thing that feels REALLY undercooked ... this mission is our pilot for everything else that matters, max priority on getting this right"). Its 24 flawless criteria (COMBAT §25.19.8) are now failing tests, all met over 100 seeds a sheet: the tier one's lane by T is 41.7 s on average and 43.1 s at most, 13.1 stops a lane with 14 at the 95th percentile, one press each; the private by the keys 71.7 s against v0.0.12's routed 78.42.
- **The rows are muscle memory by a room's phases, never a mission: "Clear the house" is gone** (your "the option to clear the whole house should not exist ... Pre-baked 'muscle memory' options should be stuff like 'stack up', 'stack up, take point', 'breach (explosive)', 'kick door'"). One press reaches one room at most, to "Room clear!". The corporal clears a room in three presses: Stack up, take point; Open, in: your corner; Dominate. A row above your rung shows greyed with its rung, and Breach (explosive) shows struck, "no charge issued".
  - Every stop says why in the line's lead ("GO.", "Contact · 0.05 s.", "Room clear. Next: room 2, 6.4 m.", "Door, east wall.", "Stoppage.", "Back on him.", "Done: …") and has a live act: dead ends over 400 lanes went from 746 to 0. A distance under 0.5 m is no longer said ("room 2, 0.0 m"), and the "Next" lead goes once you are stacked on that door.
- **A trained man flows through the door and shoots on the move: a contact no longer leaves him standing in the doorway to aim** (your "trained operators know to flow in and take shots when they're in position"). From rung 2 a contact suspends the drill instead of cutting it: the shot runs on the hands while the feet carry on to the point, and the drill goes on by itself after it. A step that needs the feet (Hold him, a stance, Wait, Stop here) still halts it.
  - A shot inside the flow is one row and its feet are the drill's: moving on for an armed man facing within 7 m, or near your path or within arm's reach; otherwise at your point when it is under 0.8 s away. Moving on, the first round waits until your feet are past the threshold, at most 0.5 s: the tier one's first round in room 1 comes 0.48 s after the sighting (0.30 before).
  - Over 400 lanes by T: doorway stops 69 lanes to 0, first rounds past the threshold 73% to 100%, "Carry on" after a row 799 to 99, stalls 7 to 0; the tier one's lane mean 47.1 s (max 137.2) to 44.3 s (max 48.6).
- **You never stop in a doorway unless you choose to, and neither does your #2** (your words of 2026-11-13: "soldiers clearing rooms know not to stop in a doorway ... soldiers need to stay fluidly moving"). The game carries a body through a door's funnel instead of braking it to rest there, on the take-in's window, on a step, on a planted shot or when the keys are let go: from rung 2 along your lane to your point, below it just clear of the door. Stop here, Hold him, Gun down and a knee are the only ways left to stand in a door, and the only ways to earn its fault. Every leafed door of the house has its funnel now, room 2's closet and the booth too, not only the doors with a plan.
  - Your #2 steps in only when you are not in his way, holds at his stack or backs out if you are, and his own brake in a door carries him through: he stood 2.1 s in room 3's doorway on 48213 before, and the fuzz's deadlocks of up to 6 minutes are gone.
  - The private by the keys stood 3.25 s in room 1's doorway; now 0 s. No watering down by the keys (F21) is met, not re-baselined: the recruit on 48213 87.2 s (106.5) against 95.2, the private 73.2 s (87.9) against 78.3, the private over 100 seeds 72.3 s (85.8) against 78.42.
  - A startle freezes your feet where they stand, never carried through a door to the point, and its freeze in a doorway is kept out of the doorway's fault.
- **"Clear!" waits until the room is clear as the team perceives it.** It holds while a man is being taken in, while your #2 is on a man of the room (at most 4 s, then "Your two's on a man."), and while a man of yours is still up. It no longer reads what nobody in the room knows (a man who turned a moment ago unseen, a turner whose trigger has fired): the instructor still voids such a call and turns the man, and the drill then says it again after the man's window, so it costs no stop.
- **Your number two is a marine as the doctrine has him.** He is issued in Condition 4 and does LOAD, MAKE READY, Standby, Stop and UNLOAD SHOW CLEAR on his own macros, and the tower waits on him (your "Partner does nothing the tower asks for"). His entry is cued once you are 0.75 m in, on his own lane, and he goes to the next stack with you, calling Ready, Clear!, Reloading!, Up and Covering!. He stacks at low ready, muzzle down, tops up on his own and covers your sector while your rifle is down.
  - On a click, a jam or an empty rifle he says "Gun down!", runs immediate action (remedial for a double feed, a speed reload when empty) on his own hands, drawn as yours, then "Gun up!" and goes back on his man. Before, he pressed a jammed rifle six times and the room was called clear with his man up.
  - You never walk through each other: your keys and the drill stop 0.50 m from his body, centre to centre (the fuzz reached 0.00 m), and every man of the lane, up or down, is a body the walker keeps off too.
  - A man in his sector is his only while he is actually on him (his take-in, his row, the man known to him, his feet on their way in, or the man in his sight): standing on his stack behind a shut door he is not, and the window pauses for you. The tier one's stops went from 12.8 to 13.1 a lane over 100 seeds.
  - His rifle counts as down when he has said "Gun down!", never by its working parts, which you cannot see.
- **A jam in a room is answered as a marine would** (your "we should be able to switch to side arm or something realistic like take cover"). The reflex card goes by the situation: with a threat you have taken in facing within 10 m and the P-12 carried, Transition: pistol, Gun down, Tap, rack (standing), Off the line, Look at the port; without a pistol Gun down leads. Each answer is one press at every rung; in a doorway the drill on the hands leads, never the knee.
  - Gun down: you take a knee, tap and rack, call "Gun up!" and stand on your #2's "Up."; he calls "Covering!" and takes your man. Off the line steps you up to 1.5 m to the wall. If his rifle goes down, one stop: "Your two's down. His sector."
  - **The P-12 is issued from the Sergeant up**, in a thigh holster with a HAD-17 of ten frangible and a spare in the thigh pouch (+1.2 kg, 25.7 to 26.9 kg). Transition takes 1.40 s × pace (the sergeant 1.05 s, the Raider 0.78 s, the tier one 0.64 s) and the pistol comes out off safe; Rifle up holsters it on safe and clears the rifle. Below the Sergeant the card shows Transition struck, "not issued".
  - With the pistol out and the man still up, the band is CONTACT's five, the pistol's shots on 1 to 5 and Rifle up in the fan; the line names To the head's keys ("Still up. Head: 4."). Before, the STOPPAGE five stood, the head row was at Tab › 1 › 5, and Fire on the band rang the plate nine times.
  - The tower's Stop and SHOW CLEAR judge both guns: a P-12 still in your hand is not safe, and the line says "Rifle up first: holster the pistol." MAKE READY racks it and SHOW CLEAR clears it, the pistol last. Before, a sergeant who finished the lane on the pistol cleared the line with a hot rifle.
  - The tier one's lane is about 2 s longer with Rifle up and the pistol's weight (40.4 to 42.5 s), and one stop more (12.9 to 13.6).
- **The step panel is grouped, every row on a fixed key** (your "group them by type ... top of the head core gameplay options being always visible and accessible (even if grayed out, like hammer if you only have one bullet)"). Five groups with fixed slots, FIRE, DRILL, GUN, BODY and TEAM, each with a third level ▸; the core five (the shot, Aim ⇄ Hold, Fix, the muzzle and NEXT, T's act) always on the same keys, so T is never the only way. The 0 pages are gone: Fire was on 43 different keys in v0.0.13 and now has one.
  - Three layouts to score in play, `&panel=a|b|c` or the Esc menu's "Step panel" (kept as `olympus.panel`); C is played until your score: the situation's five on 1 to 5, the core on 6 to 0, Tab held for the fan. In C, T's row costs 1.03 presses by its own place over 400 lanes and always one on NEXT.
  - Greyed, never removed: a struck row keeps its slot with a badge, the arrow and the fixing key, ▮ the rounds left (your Hammer ▮1), MKS for a skill gate, ▲▲ for a rung, a hand, a struck silhouette for "not issued", a padlock, a struck eye, ✓. Pressing it says why and names the key that fixes it. In the house Fire is struck on a man already down, and at the hostage card ("head only"), where a round at the torso is the shot the line forbids; a round at a man down says "he was already down".
  - The DI's keys follow the layout: at a contact in C "To the head: 4" (1 before), Breathe on 5, and the press names its key on the bays and the lines ("On him. Your shots: 1.", "Breathe out. Press: 6."). Q and D step the Watch row across the hot spots when nobody is in sight. A key the pause's guard holds now flashes the panel instead of vanishing.
- **The stances say what they trade** (your remark of 2026-11-11: "don't know what the assault <-> combat option is supposed to mean"): Careful (Combat) and Fast (Assault), each row with its walk and its moving shot in numbers (Fast: 2.0 m/s, moving shots −2). The first switch explains itself in one line, and the entry's stance row says its new stance in numbers at every press ("The entry: Careful: 1.2 m/s, moving shots −2."), its group kept open. The keys card names them the same way.
- **A skilled marine's natural 1 is caught, not a miss** (your "good defaults" of 2026-11-11, after "there should be some kind of perk of high skilled operators preventing a nat 1 from making them miss, maybe they take 50% longer"). A natural 1 at skill 15 with a 10-point margin, or on routine work, costs ×1.5 of its time and never fails; a natural 20 on timed handling is clean, ×2/3. A caught round leaves late and draws the dice its catch was judged on, even when your #2 fires in between. Shoot house misses: corporal 3.3%, sergeant 2.0%, Raider and tier one 0% (about 5% before). The panel marks `~` caught, ★ a 20, `!` a stoppage, ✗ only a failure that cost something, and "sure". Every take-in is unchanged; scene-pairs, bangs-pin, the fumble ladder and the specials' and steel's odds moved by this rule alone and are re-pinned.
- **The walk is rebuilt from the sim's own gait** (your "saying that the walking about is goofy is an understatement"). Every moving body reports one locomotion record (BODY §16.8): the gait phase in steps, the body's yaw, the flight from gravity. The #2, the instructor, the range's corpsman and the pairs coach now walk on screen (the #2 was idle on all 496 of his moving ticks). Eight frames a step with the feet planted (0.00 px of slip), the facing from the yaw with 55 degrees of hysteresis, Careful slower-legged than Fast (3.28 against 4.64 steps a second at 1 G).
  - Stops brake instead of cutting: a halted body slows at its legs' brake (the #2 from 1.64 m/s over 0.23 m), never still for a tick at a change of row, and every stop from a walk plants, a side-step's too. An aimed walk is drawn at high ready. Close quarters has its beats: the start, the plant, the side-step, the stack and its breath, the #2's tap, the hand on the door, the kick, the kneel, the shot on the move, the P-12 drawn and aimed. Each figure has its own outline, so you and your #2 in a stack are inked apart.
  - Three walk cycles sit behind one flag: the game draws B, and `&cycle=A` or `&cycle=C` shows the others until you score W-b to W-d. A save always carries B's gait phase, whichever cycle the page drew, so an exported save replays byte for byte.
  - The honest physics costs time: the tier one's lane by T 43.5 to 44.9 s over 12 seeds before the flow's gains.
- **The drill and your #2 pass the doors on their own lanes, and every authored path is walkable as drawn.** Each door path gains the door's outer middle and a threshold point 0.12 m toward the slot's turn, with corner waypoints: 7 of 15 legs cut a door frame before, 0 of 29 now (room 1's #1 path 3.08 m). Golden X4_PLANS re-set on purpose. The rifle's frame follows the walls' line, a row's round waits up to 0.5 s for its man, and the muzzle stays on him through a stoppage.
- **The muzzle flag is one 3-D rule**: the line, pitch included, against your #2's body as a capsule 0.3 m round from floor to head, out to 5 m, held 300 ms; a level aim over a kneeling or prone #2 is no fault and no "Muzzle!". Any bundle begun with the rifle aimed raises it first, and an aimed muzzle about to cross your #2 drops to low ready and comes back up, which ended the corporal's Dominate sweeping his #2 on every seed.
- **Before GO T always does what the tower waits on.** After LOAD and MAKE READY the line says what is still lacking and T does it: "Out of it now: X.", "Back on your mark." (a row that walks you back), Careful, low ready, your number two, "Standby. Still, for the GO."; at Stop and SHOW CLEAR with the pistol out, Rifle up. Before, T went silent with "The tower is waiting on you: MAKE READY." on every sheet, off the mark it looped 40 times, and the GO came only from the instructor's 60 s move-on.
- **The instructor's DI is never silent on the lane.** Out of the mode, the feet still and nothing done for 3 s, it says "Keep moving. D and S to your point, or G for the drill." (T takes you in; below rung 2, at an open door, the keys are yours). At a choice with no other line, it names the drill's next row, "Down. Next: …", and T does it. The hall's "Clear!" waits for its end, in the hall's words ("Room's done" before, 3.6 s before the hall's man turned). "Two." at a contact is now "Two in him." (your "two" is your #2), the locked door's chip says "Kick it" or "Hold the locked door", and the pairs coach's "He is down. Next: the rifle, lane one." now aims T at him. "Head only" and "Head" name To the head; your #2's man is named "his" only when there is something to carry on.
- **Every room refreshes the 30 s alertness window, so the later rooms are no longer read as unexpected.** The Raider's hall man is taken in 0.10 s and shot at 0.37 s (he missed his par on 100 of 100 seeds). The far leg is watched as you go round the bend and the corporal's muzzle points at the corner he faces: the sergeant's hostage first round 2.28 to 0.82 s, the corporal's room 1 take-in 0.50 to 0.35 s and first round 1.53 to 0.81 s. Your #2 hurries to his stack and finishes his corner looks after a man: tier one lanes with a 15th stop 24 to 1 in 100.
- **The standing order "Engage: armed, my sector" is built, offered from rung 4 and off by default** (the design's question 1, for you to try). Switched on, the tier one's stops drop from 13 to about 10 a lane, with no round on a no-shoot.
- **Smaller fixes.** Top up's "half or less" is half the magazine's own load (nine of a thirty loaded with ten led the panel from the first round). The stall clock counts progress only, so Wait pressed for ever gets "Move on." at 60 s (the lane ran to 600 s). The lane timer's par, once missed for a man, stays missed ("HE FIRED FIRST", never a fresh countdown for him seen again). The plates over a target move off the lane timer's paper. The brief's toast clears at GO. A man's room and sector are worked out from where he stands, and the reflex card's threat is a man you have taken in.
- Tests: new house-floor (7), house-flow (7), house-two (10), house-sidearm (8) and ui-sidearm, panel-groups and ui-panel, walk-cycle and ui-walk, house-bodies (6) and ui-bodies, house-catalogue, catches and catch-marks, house-through, house-r9 and house-review (11: the review findings of 2026-11-13, each failing without its fix); the flawless gauges (tests/flawless.mjs, house-flawless, -quick, -keys and -more) enforce F1 to F24. Re-pinned on purpose: enter-and-clear, house-fixes, house-muzzle, stepper, scene-shoothouse, figures-arms, game-domains, arms-kit, kit-suit, pairs-head, instinct-stress, instinct-pairs, core-browser, view-range, bench-browser and render-bench (the panel's keys: the range benches press Digit6 for Fire), scene-pairs and bangs-pin (the mastery rule), house-flow's clear gate (perceived, not the scene's), house-through's zones (five leafed doors) and walk-cycle (B's phase saved). A v0.0.13 shoot house save is added to tests/saves/.

## v0.0.13 (2026-11-11)

- **The R-7 now looks like the standard issue you picked: G2's body in the Corps' black with V1's sight** (your scores of 2026-11-08: G2 ★ 95, "Very good, make this the standard issue ... I would prefer this gun with the scope of the V1, for short and mid range engagements"). It keeps G2's skeleton stock, receivers, slotted handguard with its rail, angled foregrip, light and brake. V1's carry handle, its rail and the boxed red dot replace G2's low-power scope. The new look is the same on the weapon card, on the figure at 16 px/m in every pose and facing, on the doll and on the hit card's man. No gun's data changed.
- **The SO-4 is now drawn as a red dot with a 4× flip magnifier behind it, and the card shows which way the lever is.** A red dot alone is a 1× sight. So the SO-4's 4× is drawn as a magnifier on a flip mount (its lever, 0.5 s and the far window's numbers are unchanged). On the card it sits in line with the box when you believe the lever is at 4×, and swung 4 px down beside its mount at 1×. On the figure it is one pixel behind the box, always in line. At 300 m the turrets sit on the box: the elevation turret on its hood, the windage turret at its front.
- **The green arch is gone from the weapon card** (your "get rid of the green arch it serves no purpose", 2026-11-08). No card draws a sling now; the figure keeps its strap.
- **Rifles come in seven finishes, and a squad is issued one** (your "Make sure we have multiple colors so we can equip ourselves to camo in environments depending on deployments", 2026-11-08). They are the Corps' black (the issue's), flat dark earth, Mars rust, Earth desert, Europa ice, Earth woodland and urban grey, each a set of Mars palette colours for the furniture, the metal and the magazine.
  - Mars rust's base is the regolith's own 0xa65e4b, 1.00:1 against it, where flat dark earth is 1.54:1.
  - Earth desert's figure is drawn one step darker than its card. At 16 px/m it read as near white beside Europa ice (1.01:1); it is now a warm sand at 1.47:1 against ice's cool grey.
  - A gun item carries `finish` only when it is not the issue's black, so every save and scene keeps its bytes, and a save from before finishes loads with its rifles in black.
  - The weapon card and the figure draw the issued rifle's own finish, read from the item and never from what you believe.
  - Every scene issues black for now.
- **G0 is purged, G1 and V1 are kept as designs, and M1 is the R-7M's drawing** (your scores of 2026-11-08: G0 "Purge it from used assets it's too low quality", G1 ★ 90 "not R-7 replacement", V1 ★ 95 "keep it also", M1 ★ 95 "Long gun standard for marksmanship for sure"). The R-7 as v0.0.9 drew it is gone from the card, from the figure's template and from the samples. V1 now has a black sample.
- **The hit card is the khaki man-target board at every window size, and the board shrinks with the target's range** (your scores of 2026-11-08: B ★ 99, "Second set is perfect, keep all three to represent varying ranges"; A 10, C 0). The doll-in-colour and casualty-card looks are gone.
  - The board's figure is drawn at 96 px a metre up to 10 m (the card as you scored it, unchanged to the pixel), 80 px up to 25 m and 64 px beyond. A farther board stands lower on longer stakes, and the marks keep their sizes.
  - The game takes the range from the line you shoot from ("Bang"'s 7 m line, for every lane), or from the ground between you and the target where there is no line. So "Bang"'s cards are as before.
- **The HAD-30 on the card is the standard issue's magazine as you scored it with G2.** It is 9 px wide (7 px from v0.0.6 to v0.0.11, after your "if they could be a bit slicker" of 2026-11-07) and curves 5 px (8 before). Its window is 2 px and its ribs run across its lower third. It still shows 0.165 m below the well. The later pick wins (my call); setting rifles.js MAG_W_PX back to 7 undoes it.
- **In the shoot house, the drill and your #2 no longer walk through walls** (your report of 2026-11-11: "Operators path through walls when automatic pathing"). Every automated move is now routed by the nav on the house's grid, at the clearance your own walk keeps (the C0 class, 0.27 m; C1's 0.33 m cannot pass the 0.9 m doors on the 0.25 m grid). The house is otherwise as v0.0.12: its rework is designed and comes next.
  - A door counts as shut while closed or swinging, except the one the bundle opens itself, so room 2's locked closet is walked round, never through.
  - The routing covers the approach to the next stack, the entry, the far leg, "Carry on", "Move to your point", "Move to the hall's end" and the feet under a shot fired on the move, whose stopping distance now ends at the wall. A leg already clear is kept as drawn; a leg that cuts a wall corner gets a waypoint 0.005 m beyond the walker's clearance.
  - "Carry on" picks up the path from where you stand, instead of walking back to a point behind you.
  - Before, every lane had 13 to 20 places where a body's centre was 0.26 to 0.35 m into a wall's 0.27 m clearance: 7 of the house's 15 authored legs cut a wall as drawn (every stack-to-threshold leg cut its door frame), and the #2 went through room 2's west wall. Now there are none in 600 lanes over 100 seeds and every sheet. The plan's data is unchanged.
  - The routes are longer than the old straight lines. Reference room 1 to your own "Clear!": the tier one 5.27 s (5.11 before; the target 4.98), the Raider 5.48 s (5.29); the recruit, private, corporal and sergeant unchanged. The lanes on 48213 are 0.5 to 7.5 s longer: the recruit 95.2 s (87.7), the private 78.3 s (73.5), the tier one 46.4 s (44.0), the private's still 1.69 times the tier one's.
- **Low ready over your #2 is no longer a muzzle fault, and "Carry on" raises the rifle before it walks** (your report of 2026-11-11: "tier one operator automatically goes low ready when sweeping their partner (which is good) but we still get flagged a fault for muzzle"). The rifle was still aimed when it swept him. After a shot, "Carry on" raised the muzzle first only when the rest of the bundle held no posture beat, and a lane's rest always holds the next room's own high ready, which waits until 60% of the approach. So the tier one walked out of room 2 aimed across his still #2 for 2.35 s, the fault coming 350 ms before the rifle came up, while the figure drew the aimed walk at low ready, which is what you saw. Now every carry that starts aimed brings the muzzle up first.
  - One rule (carry.js `flagsAt` and `SWEEP_SAFE`) now serves the house's fault and the instructor's "Muzzle!": high ready and low ready pass over him (my call, reading your words), and aimed through him is still the fault after 300 ms. The two flag distances (0.3 m and 5 m) are read from the house course's data, no longer defined twice.
  - Muzzle faults by T and the house bot: 6 of 20 lanes before (the Raider on 4 of 5 seeds, the tier one on your seed 1489397426), 0 of 20 now.
- **T no longer stalls in the shoot house with one round left.** With every shot row greyed out ("one left"), the instructor still said "Pair." with nothing to press, so T did nothing and the lane ended on time after 600 s. Now his line is "Aim" then "Press" on the Fire row, as below rung 2, and with the rifle empty "Reload." leads. The Raider on seed 101 and the tier one on your seed 1489397426 now reach the card by T alone.
- **A man in room 3's far leg no longer stops you looking down it.** A contact that cut the look down the far leg counted it done, though your eyes were on the man, not on the leg: on 48213 the tier one's ended 20.6° off the leg's middle (the limit is 20°), and the instructor said "Corner! You never looked." The look is now owed until done, and "Carry on" does it.
- **The step panel's three layouts are in the gallery to score** (your ask of 2026-11-11: "If We're going to keep this system of numbered options, we need to find a way to group them by type and find a clever way to make this manageable with top of the head core gameplay options being always visible and accessible (even if grayed out, like hammer if you only have one bullet)"). The panel sample (`./play.sh --gallery`), nothing changed in play. Every row has a fixed group and slot (FIRE, DRILL, GUN, BODY, TEAM), the core sits on fixed keys, and a row the situation could offer is never removed: it is greyed, with a badge saying why and pointing to the fix (a keycap, ▮ and a count of rounds, MKS, ▲▲ and a rung, a struck silhouette, a struck eye; ✓ already done, my call).
  - P1, "Home and drawers": the core on 1 to 5 (the shot, Aim ⇄ Hold, Fix, Muzzle, NEXT with its T keycap), FIRE, DRILL, GUN, BODY and TEAM as drawers on 6 to 0. FIRE opens by itself at a contact's pause but stays shut with a stoppage, so Fix is one press (my call: the proposal does not say). The DI's line names the path ("7 › 3"). Open view: DRILL's drawer outside a door.
  - P2, "The fixed ten and the letter banks": the shots on 1 to 5 and the core on 6 to 0 in every scene, Hammer on 2, struck ▮1 with one round left; banks R, C, B and F at STEPS' right edge with their live counts; the DI's line "R › 3". Open view: bank R, Esc to close.
  - P3, "Five that follow the situation": 1 to 5 the situation's five, named at the band's left (AT THE DOOR, CONTACT, STOPPAGE, THE TOWER), 6 to 0 the core, a Tab keycap opening the rest. Open view: the Tab fan, a half-wheel inside STEPS with DRILL picked and its nine slots.

Pull."), a corporal at the hall's pistol man with one round left ("Press."), the corporal's click on the dummy in room 2 ("Immediate action."), the 25 m line under LOAD, and the open view. Each at 1600 × 900 (--ui 1.25, verbs and times in full) and 1024 × 768 (--ui 0.85, one word a key, the times left to the hover): 10 views a layout. The rows come from the game's own stepsOf with its times and odds; the rows the proposal adds (Stack up, take point; Breach; Flash; Fix; Stop here; the TEAM rows; Transition: pistol) are drawn in the state it gives them, with no time.

Live (O): Space stops and runs time, S slows it to a quarter, F hides the footprints, R starts again.

Seen from the front or behind every candidate's feet still slide 5 to 11 px a step (W0's 4 to 7), since the stride is drawn at 0.4 of its length there.
